```
The credit score provided in CreditWise is a FICO® Score 8 based on TransUnion® data. The FICO Score 8 gives you a good sense of your credit health, but it may not be the same score model used by your lender or creditor. The availability of the CreditWise tool and certain features in the tool depends on our ability to obtain your credit history from TransUnion and whether you have sufficient credit history to generate a FICO Score 8. you if the information you enter at enrollment does not match the information in your credit file at (or you do not have a file at) one or more consumer reporting agencies.
```

```
Regards,

Mr. Alexandria Kurt
```

```
Best regards, Erich KellerInvestment Broker | Project Finance Consultant.
```

```
Hi,

 

Do you need a new Website or want to improve your current one? I am a
graphic designer / web developer from India, ready to provide customized
solutions for your business.

 

I'd love to share my portfolio and work with you to create something that
fits your needs perfectly. Share your ideas, or let me know your phone
number for quick communication.

 

Looking forward to connecting with you!

 

Best regards,

Shakti,

 

P.S.: If you'd prefer not to receive further emails from me, simply reply
with "NO" in the subject line.
```
